# CategoryTheory.ringedHomUnitEvaluationIso

## Reason

Delivery path: `coord/changes/E6-coefficient-hom-unit-evaluation.md`.
The stage-3 review-fix interface uses the flat filename
`E6-coefficient-hom-unit-evaluation.change.md` for a correction to this record
(`tools/batch_land.py:fixes`). It does not accept the ordinary `.md` basename.
The returned packet's independent review passed, but its landing failed while
applying a correction under `E6-coefficient-hom-unit-evaluation.md`.

On rebase, retain the current `ringedCompletionHasSheafCompose` theorem, its
local instance priority, the existing three evaluation component/naturality
lemmas, and their private proof import. These current-main additions retain their
original signatures alongside the repaired representation below. The two component proofs use the proved
evaluation API because the new component definition is locally irreducible;
the naturality proof and sheaf-composition helper remain unchanged.

The coefficient-Hom unit proof must identify each evaluation component with the
actual `homComplexπ` projection. With the previous definition, checking that
conversion unfolds a large chosen Hom-complex construction. Profiling measured
13 seconds of kernel time for that single component equality, and repeated
conversion made the item exceed the file time limit. Construct the same component
using the projection formula directly, and retain its identification with the
original evaluation map as a named theorem. This changes data representation,
not the mathematical map. The supporting module and definition node are unclaimed.

The concrete sheaf declarations locally prefer `hasSheafCompose_of_preservesLimitsOfSize` over the multicospan instance. Both prove exactly `HasSheafCompose`; this changes the proof found by instance search and preserves the underlying ring sheaf.

## Old

```lean
def ringedHomUnitEvaluationIso (M : ringedSiteComplex O) :
    homComplex (R := O) (ringedSiteRingComplex O) M ≅ M :=
  HomologicalComplex.Hom.isoOfComponents
    (fun n ↦ @asIso _ _ _ _ (homComplexUnitEvaluation J O M n)
      (homComplex_unit_evaluation_isIso J O M n))
    (fun n m h ↦ homComplex_unit_evaluation_comm J O M n m h)
```

## New

```lean
def ringedHomUnitEvaluationIso (M : ringedSiteComplex O) :
    homComplex (R := O) (ringedSiteRingComplex O) M ≅ M :=
  HomologicalComplex.Hom.isoOfComponents
    (fun n ↦ @asIso _ _ _ _ (ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap O M n)
      (ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap_isIso O M n))
    (fun n m h ↦ ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap_comm O M n m h)
```

The return type and parameters are unchanged.

`ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap` is the actual source-degree-zero projection followed
by `homObjMap` of the single-complex identification and `homObjUnitIso.hom`.
`ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap_eq` proves by `rfl` that it is the old component.
The named `ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap_isIso` and
`ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap_comm` transport the existing proved properties.
The private theorem `ringedHomUnitEvaluationIso_eq_previous` proves equality of
the new isomorphism with the entire previous construction, by component
extensionality. Thus dependent definitions and statements retain exactly their
values and meaning. No target signature changes. Target statement review is
reopened wherever this definition is used, as required by the definition-change
rule; the equality certificate supplies the semantic check.

The additional `ringedHomUnitFunctorEvaluationIso` presents the same isomorphism
with source `((homComplexFunctor (R := O)).obj (op (ringedSiteRingComplex O))).obj M`.
It is defined to be `ringedHomUnitEvaluationIso O M`, using their definitionally
equal endpoints. The two public API theorems prove by reflexivity that its inverse
is the original inverse and its forward components are `ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap`.
Only the assigned unit module consumes this new presentation. Existing signatures
and values remain as certified above.

All existing public imports are retained. The added public import of
`WeilConjectures.Algebra.Homology.HomComplexComponents` supplies the projection
used in the new public definition. No adopted source-library file changes.

The proved `ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap_normalized` removes precomposition by
the identity at literal degree zero. The actual component definition retains
the preceding formula, and `ringedHomUnitEvaluationMap_eq` remains reflexivity.
This preserves the definitional equality used by the existing consumer
`RingedHomTensorUnitNormalization.unit_evaluation_f`. The simplified formula
is used only through its proved API; no consumer adaptation is required.

--uses ringedHomUnitEvaluationIso`. For each, the entire isomorphism has the same
value by the equality certificate, so all downstream statement meanings are
unchanged. Claims were read on 2026-10-26.

## Status

landed 20261026T111828-L-1744881 2026-10-26 (merge of deliver/E6/Sheaf.Paper.CoefficientHomUnit)

The three new functor-presentation declarations abstract the propositional
`HasSheafCompose` instance. Their value and inverse are still the original
isomorphism, as proved by the inverse equation. This lets consumers retain a
single instance proof throughout their comparison diagram; the original
evaluation-isomorphism declaration and its explicit binders are unchanged.
